QHCF / Carrion Fields preservation reference

Historical ranking and item references

Historical community material. Ranking advice and equipment statistics are not current-game claims unless separately live-verified and dated.

These source families stay separate: ranger ranking, class-ranking navigation, general ranking, good/evil equipment advice, the Item List index/glossary, and the compiled carritems text dump.

carritems.txt

compiled item text dump - The data file linked by Item List. Kept distinct from the navigation/glossary page and from alignment-specific ranking equipment advice.

Archived 2025 compiled dump; item fields are preserved source claims, not verified current equipment stats.

Snapshot
2025-08-16
